# Switch inventory -- AthenaK rt-integration @ ed188f69 (2026-09-22)

Read-only inventory of every runtime parameter read by the RT / convection / well-balanced /
cubed-sphere code paths, for a per-switch decision: **ABANDON / DEFAULT ON / KEEP**.
Machine-readable twin: `docs/dev/switch_inventory_2026-09-22.csv` (same rows, one per parameter).

Production inputs the "set in" columns are taken from:
- `cs_mhd_prod3` = `bench/cs_mhd_prod3/deep_hot_jupiter.athinput`
- `sp_mhd_prod3` = `bench/sp_mhd_prod3/deep_hot_jupiter.athinput`
- `bstar_prod_w7` = `bench/bstar_fecz/prod_w7/fecz_rt_w7.athinput`
- `he_box_w8` = `bench/hestar_fecz/box_w8/he_box_w8.athinput`
- `RG_fofc_long3` = `bench/RG_fofc_long3/rg.athinput`
- `m1_slab_V3edd` = `tests_m1/runs_3d_edd/he_slab_m1_2d_V3edd.athinput`

## Counts

| verdict | count |
| --- | --- |
| ABANDON | 99 |
| DEFAULT ON | 13 |
| KEEP | 284 |
| UNSURE | 36 |
| **total** | **432** |

## The 15 switches whose removal or default change would simplify the most

Ordered by what the change buys. "Bitwise" statements are the agents' readings of the code, not a
measured diff — verify before flipping anything.

### Default changes (7)

| # | switch | now | proposed | what it buys / what changes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `problem/rt_split` | false | **true** | Bitwise inert for all 6 productions (`rt_ck` and `rt_grey` already force it true). Flipping it makes the ~394-line monolithic sweep in `two_stream_rt.hpp:5986-6379` formally unreachable and deletable. |
| 2 | `problem/ck_spherical` | false | **true** | The plane-parallel divergence lets L = A F grow like r^2 through a transparent shell on the r_out/r_in = 2.18 dhj grid. `tests_ck_sph`: conservation gap 1.80e-2 -> 2.05e-10. Changes every ck run bitwise. HEAD ed188f69 just repaired the `false` path, so both are currently correct. |
| 3 | `problem/ck_beam_sph` | false | **true** | Plane-parallel caps the secant law at 10x the vertical depth and leaves the whole twilight region dark; costs 1.08-1.12x. Changes every ck run bitwise. |
| 4 | `hydro/wb_cache_every` + `mhd/wb_cache_every` | 0 | **1** | 1 is the safe fast setting; 10 grew a spurious f-mode (gamma 0.86 -> 0.05 when removed). NOT bitwise: stages 2 and 3 then reconstruct against the stage-1 background. Correction to the memory note: `bstar_w7` and `he_box_w8` already carry **0**; only `sp_prod3` and `RG_long3` still carry **10** — those two are the ones to fix, in the input. |
| 5 | `rad_m1/implicit_gas_newton` | false | **true** | The gas-radiation energy coupling is solved by Newton; 20 fallbacks in 3.29e6 cell-passes. |
| 6 | `rad_m1/f_source` | cell | **wb** | The F source is evaluated at faces, killing the spurious hydrostatic residual. |
| 7 | `problem/rt_use_cons` | false (dhj only) | **true** | `box_convection` already defaults it true and `red_giant` ties it to mode 3; only `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp` defaults false. Flipping moves `sp_prod3` bitwise. |

Also proposed DEFAULT ON by the slices, smaller blast radius: `problem/grav_point_mass`,
`problem/rot_potential` (both dhj productions set them; the current defaults are the historical,
wrong-for-this-grid values), `problem/rt_col3_ex_iter`, `problem/mlt_split_deposit`,
`rad_m1/implicit_closure_lag = step`.

### Removals (8), by lines freed

| # | switch / family | est. lines | why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 8 | M1 `implicit_accel=anderson` + `implicit_anderson_*` | ~400 | FAILED: "ANDERSON DOES NOT CONVERGE THE OPTICALLY THIN TOP" (runs_3e_newton). |
| 9 | M1 `implicit_flux != central` + the six `implicit_blend*` keys | ~350 | Unreachable — `transport = implicit` fatals on anything but `central`. Also frees the unconditionally allocated `ifw` device array. |
| 10 | `box_convection` RT operator-split family (`rt_strang`, `rt_imex`, `rt_pair_sym`, `rt_before_flux`, `rt_once_per_cycle`, `rt_col3_once`, `rt_split_transverse`) | ~385 | Every one a refuted/rejected/WIP way of moving the same source out of the RK stage; no production sets any. |
| 11 | `box_convection` f-mode campaign (`seed_fmode_*`, `fmode_hist`, `work_hist`) | ~340 | Campaign retracted ("THE SEEDED LINEAR RADIATING BOX DOES NOT GROW"). Frees history slots 5-15, which the `<rad_m1>` path is currently blocked on. |
| 12 | `rt_implicit_column` modes 1 and 2 | ~350-450 | Not even distinguishable from each other (no `== 1`/`== 2` test exists anywhere); mode 3 is what both stellar productions run. Removal also deletes the `skip_de`/`wthk_` blend from `rt_apply`. |
| 13 | `problem/runaway_scan` + `src/utils/runaway_scan.hpp` | ~310 | Debugging instrument for a closed question; header not even in `src/CMakeLists.txt`. |
| 14 | `mhd/cs_seam_econsist` (~235) + `mhd/cs_seam_diag` (~225) | ~460 | One measured marginally worse and fatal on >1 rank; the other instruments a RETRACTED hypothesis. Two whole files. |
| 15 | `rt_layer_legacy` (~305) + the picket-fence kernels (~322 split, ~394 monolithic) + `deep_hot_jupiter_rt_old.cpp` (2123) | ~3100 | All unreachable from any production or gate. `get_picket_fence_coeff` must survive (the photosphere dump uses it); `deep_hot_jupiter_rt_old.cpp` is an orphan file nothing references. |

Honourable mentions just below the line: `wellbalance_static` MHD half (~265 lines, no gate and no
production at all), `conduction`'s `rad_blend_use_2s` (~175, REFUTED and fatal with
`rad_implicit_x1`), `rad_x1_verbose`/`rad_x1_every` (~170, finished audit), `cs_lowbeta_diag` (~110,
and the only reason the low-beta rsolver carries four extra kernel arguments),
`use_polar_average_b` (~100, measured to do nothing).

# DEAD CODE PATHS

Code paths reachable only through a switch nobody sets. Line ranges and counts are the reviewing
agents' estimates from grep site maps plus spot reads, not from an actual deletion; several of these
branches carry long in-line comment blocks that hold the measurements, so read them before cutting.

### Two-stream RT core (`src/utils/two_stream*.hpp`, `correlated_k.hpp`) + `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp`

**(a) `rt_split = false`, the monolithic sweep — DEAD. ~394 lines.**
`two_stream_rt.hpp:5986-6379` (the tail of `picket_fence_two_stream_RT_pass` after the
`if (rt_split) { … return; }` block that closes at `:5985`), plus the commented-out
`par_for_outer("2stream_rt", …)` remnant at `:5987-6013`.
Production runs the SPLIT path, always: `rt_ck` forces `rt_split = true`
(`deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:535-543`, gated by `tst/test_suite/rad/test_rad_dhj_ck_cpu.py:78`) and
`rt_grey` sets it directly (`box_convection.cpp:2208`, `red_giant.cpp:1780`). No production input
and no gate reaches the monolithic branch; the grey arm was explicitly removed from the one test
that used to reach it (`tst/test_suite/rad/test_rad_dhj_srclim_cpu.py:77-82`).

**(b) `rt_implicit_column` modes 0/1/2 vs 3 — modes 1 and 2 are dead, and are not even
distinguishable.** `grep -rn "rt_implicit_column *== *1\|== *2\|> *1" src/` returns nothing: the
only tests in the tree are `== 3` and `(> 0 && != 3)`, so modes 1 and 2 are the *same* code path,
selected by `implcol_` (`two_stream_rt.hpp:2282`). The header says mode 3 does "what modes 1 and 2
could not do" (`:782-790`). bstar_w7 and he_box_w8 run **mode 3**; nothing runs 1 or 2, and
`deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:540-552` fatals on any non-zero value.
Mode-1/2 footprint (`implcol_`-only): `two_stream_rt.hpp:2280-2342` (setup + Jacobian array
binding), `:2840-2870` and `:3065-3267` (Jacobian accumulation in the grey chain), `:4562-4600`
(same in the picket chain), `:5140-5200` (residual/Jacobian handover), `:5593-5670` (the `(1-w)`
split apply); plus the `rtc_` branch of `Conduction::ImplicitRadialUpdate`,
`src/diffusion/conduction.cpp:1923-2000` and its in-kernel rows. **~350-450 lines**, and the removal
simplifies `rt_apply` (the `skip_de`/`wthk_` blend disappears, `mode3_` stays). The ranges are where
the code lives, not a clean contiguous excision.

**(c) The picket-fence kernel — DEAD (both copies).**
The grey 3-channel Guillot/picket-fence scheme is reached only when `!rt_ck && !rt_grey`. Two
implementations exist: the split version `par_for("rt_chain", …)` at
`two_stream_rt.hpp:4559-~4880` (**~322 lines**) and the monolithic version of (a) (**~394 lines**);
shared: `get_picket_fence_coeff` (`:1380-1480`, ~100 lines) and its call in `rt_pre` (`:2667`).
No production sets it (cs_prod3/sp_prod3 -> `rt_ck`; bstar_w7/he_box_w8/RG_long3 -> `rt_grey`), and
no gate. **`get_picket_fence_coeff` must survive** even if the kernels go — the photosphere dump
(`deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:3805`) is a live consumer.

**(d) Legacy flags.**
- `rt_cut_bc_legacy` (`two_stream_rt.hpp:899`; read at `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:486`,
  `box_convection.cpp:2231`, `red_giant.cpp:1827`): pre-fix reproduction only; the old behaviour is
  documented as a bug (F_2s/F_diff = 2.0002). ~20 conditional lines at `:2805,3054,3176,3523`.
  Zero hits in `tests_*/` and `docs/dev/`.
- `rt_layer_legacy` (`:950`): the staggered whole-cell layers. Three duplicated legacy branches —
  `:2974-3044` (grey, ~70), `:3727-3872` (ck, ~145), `:4621-4710` (picket, ~90) = **~305 lines** —
  plus five fatal guards that exist only to refuse it (`:1572,1617,1719,1750,1784`). Refused by
  `ck_implicit`, by `ck_spherical` and by mode 3. Nothing sets it.
- `rt_semi_lin` (`:478`, default TRUE = the OLD linearization) and `rt_explicit` (`:503`):
  `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp` never reads either, so on this pgen they are frozen at their compiled-in
  values; they are live only through red_giant / box_convection.
- **Bonus: `src/pgen/deep_hot_jupiter_rt_old.cpp`, 2123 lines** — an entire legacy copy of this
  problem generator. Not in `src/CMakeLists.txt`; buildable only via
  `-D PROBLEM=deep_hot_jupiter_rt_old`; nothing references it.

**`inline` globals in `two_stream_rt.hpp` never assigned from any `pin->` read** (all 89
scalar/string globals checked against every assignment in `src/pgen/*.cpp`):
- Internal run-time counters / one-shot latches, **not switches**, keep: `rt_nclip_last`,
  `rt_c3_lines`, `rt_nanrep_lines`, `rt_srclim_warned`, `rt_eiclamp_warned`, `rt_stclamp_warned`,
  `rt_dump_done`.
- `rt_nanrep_maxlines = 400` — a genuine tunable exposed by no input key. Expose it or leave it.
- **`rt_plane_parallel`** (`:345`) — hard-wired `true` at `box_convection.cpp:2209`, `false`
  everywhere else; no `pin->` read anywhere. A pgen property, not a user switch; the only thing it
  gates that a user can reach is the `rt_layer_legacy` curvilinear fatal at `:1719`.
- Everything else (`rt_grey`, `rt_diag`, `rt_bot_flux`, `rt_force_grav`, `rt_tint_override`,
  `rt_impl_solver`) is assigned from a pin-derived quantity even though the assignment line carries
  no `pin->`, so none is an ABANDON candidate on this criterion.

Also noted: `rt_src_direct` defaults false and `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp` never reads it, so every
dhj correlated-k run forms the source as a flux difference rather than directly
(`tests_ck_sph/README.md` 0.1). Same for `rt_bface`, `rt_newton`, `rt_rescue_eq`, `rt_top_clamp`,
`rt_top_re`, `rt_grey`, `rt_outer_iter`, `rt_nan_report`, `rt_rad_force` — dhj-inaccessible, but
live in the other pgens, so not dead.

### Radiative conduction / ADI / STS (`src/diffusion/conduction*.cpp`)

Line counts are estimates from grep site maps plus spot reads. Several of these paths carry long
in-line comment blocks that hold the measurements — read them before deleting.

**A. `rad_blend_use_2s` — FULLY DEAD, REFUTED. ~175 lines (~95 code + ~80 comment).**
`conduction.cpp:161-167` (read + range validation), `:308-333` (`rad_f2s` alloc + 3 fatals),
`:437-445` (`rad_sts_all` fatal), `:1536-1565` (`use2s`/`pres2s` setup + the prescribed-flux branch
of `radcond1`), `:1913-1915`, `:2045-2060` (zero-conductance mask in `radimpx1_face`);
`conduction.hpp:662-743`; plus `utils/two_stream_rt.hpp:4981-4988` and
`utils/two_stream_column_implicit.hpp:1506`. Not merely unused: `conduction.cpp:324-332` makes it a
startup fatal with `rad_implicit_x1`, which 4 of the 6 productions set, so it is unreachable in any
production configuration. Handover: "leave `rad_blend_use_2s` behind as refuted".

**B. `rad_x1_verbose` / `rad_x1_every` — diagnostic-only, no gate. ~170 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:263-265`, `:1919-1921` (`x1dbg_`), `:1994`, `:2284`, and the audit itself
`:2396-2550` (the T-linearisation error kernel + per-face report), `:2533-2534`. The audit it
existed to run is finished; its conclusion shipped as `rad_x1_uform`/`rad_x1_kiter`, which are
gated and in production.

**C. `rad_adi_scheme = douglas` (`ADISCM_DOUGLAS`) + `rad_adi_theta`. ~74 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:230-233`, `:537-548` (theta validation, which exists only to refuse theta with lod),
`:604`; `conduction.hpp:374-380,407-412,427,432,435`; `conduction_transverse.cpp:608,612`,
`:689-711` (comment with the measurement), `:826-827`, `:836-843`, `:1337-1342`. Measured failure:
"does essentially NOTHING to the checkerboard" (0.3 % of the needed relaxation at the B-star's
z~1500); the 1-rank gate arm dies at cycle 3411 with transverse KE 2e4x the RKL1 arm's.

**D. `rad_adi_scheme = lod2a` (`ADISCM_LOD2A`). ~19 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:237-238,606,609`; `conduction.hpp:431`; `conduction_transverse.cpp:735-741`
(comment), `:764,773,787,830-831,1203,1339`. Measured worse than `lod2` — alternating the half-step
order breaks the Richardson extrapolation.

**E. `rad_adi_scheme = lodn` + `rad_adi_nsub`. ~30 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:234,239-241,248-251` (lod<->lodn promotion/demotion), `:607,610`;
`conduction.hpp:419,430,434`; `conduction_transverse.cpp:721-728` (comment), `:766-767,789-795`.
In-code: "the honest brute-force fallback, and the comparison the extrapolation has to beat" — the
comparison was made, `lod2` won.

**F. `rad_adi_cross_iter > 1` (cubed sphere only, never set). ~37 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:255-256,705` (`tr_ylg` alloc); `conduction.hpp:436-442,451`;
`conduction_transverse.cpp:796-820` (the `cit > 0` lagged-answer exchange: deep_copy +
InitRecv/PackAndSend/Recv spin/fence/Clear), `:860`. Only the `cit > 0` body is unreachable; the
`for (cit…)` wrapper with `ncrit = 1` stays. Removing it also frees the `tr_ylg` 5-D register.

**G. `rad_adi_seam_w != 0.5`. ~12 lines.** `conduction.cpp:257-262` (read + range fatal);
`conduction.hpp:443-447`; `conduction_transverse.cpp:1251`. The panel-seam sub-step itself is alive;
only the switch is dead — `w = 1/2` is argued in-code to be the only correct value.

**H. `rad_tr_halo_faces_only`. ~40 lines in `conduction*`, plus `skip_x2x3_diag` in bvals.**
`conduction.cpp:191-192,395-401` (cubed-sphere fatal), `:735-751` (auto-disable when
`rad_tr_halo_every > 1`); `conduction.hpp:598-606`; `conduction_transverse.cpp:174`; plus
`bvals/bvals.hpp:124,258` and the diagonal-skip branches in the CC packing (not line-counted).
Legal only at `rad_tr_halo_every == 1`, fatal on the cubed sphere, set by nothing.

**I. `rad_tr_window = false` branch. ~10 lines.** `conduction.cpp:190`;
`conduction_transverse.cpp:311-312,807,1449-1451` — ternaries that collapse to plain `is`/`ie`.
The windowed path is what every run uses; the diagnostic build that exercised `false`
(`wt_m3acc/build_hip_trwin`, 1327a8a9) is recorded as superseded. The *parameter* stays (the default
is load-bearing); the `false` arm is dead.

**J. `rad_sts_margin != 0.10`. ~15 lines.** `conduction.cpp:188,492-495,773`;
`conduction_transverse.cpp:1357-1361,1378`; `conduction.hpp:557-562`. Both gate inputs carry the
default literally; nothing varies it. Hard-code 0.10.

**K. `rad_flim_legacy`. ~25 lines.** `conduction.cpp:120-122`; `conduction.hpp:60-76`. Its whole
effect is the constant `rad_flim_fac`, threaded as the kernel argument `ffac` through `RadFaceKCode`
(`conduction.cpp:954-981`) and four call sites (`:1021,1452,1901,2928`). Deleting it hard-codes 4.0
and drops one device-lambda capture on the hottest radiative kernel.

**L. `rad_pcut_bar` (the pre-blend pressure cut). ~15 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:109,1017,1441,1897,2916`; `conduction.hpp:48-53`; read back by
`pgen/red_giant.cpp:3507`. Superseded by the tau blend; all 6 productions use `rad_tau_lo/hi`.

**M. `rad_tmax_kappa`. ~12 lines.** `conduction.cpp:127-131,1016,1439,1896,2915`;
`conduction.hpp:84-90`. A dt-rescue experiment (5c58426e), bitwise inert at 0, never adopted.

**N. `rad_kappa_rmax` (+ its half of `rad_kappa_above`). ~25 lines.**
`conduction.cpp:133,137-142` (mutual-exclusion fatal with `rad_gate_rho`), `:855-857,1028`,
`:1463-1466,1902,2964`; `conduction.hpp:157-170`; plus 8 sites in `utils/two_stream_rt.hpp`.
Superseded by `rad_gate_rho`. **`rad_kappa_above` must survive** — it is also the floor opacity of
the `rad_gate_rho` blend.

**NOT dead despite having no production** (checked): `rad_sts_all`, `rad_sts_split`,
`rad_sts_split_x`, `rad_sts_once` all have real CI gates
(`tst/test_suite/rad/test_rad_sts_all_cpu.py`, `test_rad_sts_split_cpu.py`, plus
`inputs/tests/rad_sts_all_gauss.athinput`, `rad_transverse_gauss.athinput`). `rad_sts_perplane` is
set true by `he_box_w8`, and its `perpl` machinery is shared with the ADI path
(`perpl = (rad_sts_perplane || adi) && !sts1`, `conduction_transverse.cpp:213`), so deleting the
switch would not delete the code.

### M1 radiation module (`src/rad_m1/`, 7776 lines) — module is PAUSED, listed not pruned

Line ranges are from the working tree at ed188f69 and are approximate at the +-3-line level where a
branch is interleaved with a live one.

**A. Anderson acceleration — `implicit_accel=anderson`, `implicit_anderson_m/beta/start`.
~400 lines. FAILED** (`tests_m1/runs_3e_newton/RESULTS.txt` N-c: "ANDERSON DOES NOT CONVERGE THE
OPTICALLY THIN TOP -- FAIL").
`rad_m1_implicit.cpp:1879-1938` (M1GlobalSumArr + M1SolveSPD, called only from ImplicitAccelApply)
~60; `:1951-2157` (ImplicitAccelSave/Apply) 207; `:182-215` (parse + 4 fatals) 34; `:521-538`
(aa_xc/aa_fc/aa_xp/aa_fp/aa_dx/aa_df/aa_sc alloc, 2 six-D Views) 18; `:574-580` and `:2403-2410`
15; `:2710` + the Save/Apply call sites in the Picard loop ~10; `rad_m1.cpp:118-127` 10;
`rad_m1.hpp:419-440,526-533` 30; `rad_m1_implicit.hpp:110-125` 16.

**B. `implicit_flux != central` (ap_hll | berthon | blend) + all six `implicit_blend*` keys.
~350 lines.** Unreachable in the production mode: `transport = implicit` fatals on anything but
central (`rad_m1_implicit.cpp:240`), so only the legacy 1-D implicit_x1 mode could take it, and no
run or gate does.
`rad_m1_implicit.cpp:2940-3115` (the "(b2)" face-coefficient block, kernel `m1_impl_aphll`, shared
with C) ~176; `:3240-3310` ~35; `:3555-3570` ~12; `:222-280` (parse) 59; `:550-557` 8;
`rad_m1_implicit.hpp:126-190` ~65.
NOTE: the `ifw` device array (M1_NIFW x nmb x ncells3 x ncells2 x ncells1+1) is allocated
UNCONDITIONALLY at `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:454-455` and stays identically zero under `central` —
removing B frees that memory on every implicit run.

**C. `implicit_recon = plm_dc` + `implicit_recon_w` / `implicit_res_floor` /
`implicit_recon_lag` / `implicit_recon_npass`. ~100 lines beyond B. FAILED** — the plm limiter
makes the Picard loop a limit cycle that never meets `implicit_tol` (runs_3a2, runs_3c).
Shares the (b2) block of B (the `dodg` deferred-correction half, ~60 of those 176 lines, flags
plmdc/rwin/rfl_/rfreeze/rnpass at `:2484-2488`); `:282-320` (parse + recon_lag fatal) 38;
`rad_m1_implicit.hpp:193` ~8.

**D. `closure = minerbo | kershaw`. ~30 lines.** Unstable; `closure` is exercised only as
`m1 | eddington`. `rad_m1_closure.hpp:41-50,56-61` 16; `rad_m1.cpp:413-419,427-432` 14. `chi_kind`
then collapses to a compile-time constant and drops out of `M1Chi`'s signature and 3 call sites.

**E. `thick_flux = scaled` + `scaled_prefactor`. ~34 lines.** Set by no run, no gate; `ap_hll` is
the surviving AP form. `rad_m1_closure.hpp:30,~205-210,287-300` 22; `rad_m1.cpp:151-154,173-180` 12.

**F. `ap_form = unified`. ~25 lines.** Measurably worse on every discriminating 1b gate
(`tests_m1/runs_1c/RESULTS.txt`). `rad_m1_closure.hpp:34,341-352` 14; `rad_m1.cpp:163-173` 11.
The `apform` argument then drops out of `M1HLLFlux`'s signature.

**G. `implicit_trans_limit = lp` + `implicit_trans_fmax`. ~195 lines + one halo slot.** PARTIAL
success only: "the limiter only caps the instability" (`tests_m1/runs_3b8/RESULTS.txt`); the slab
runs with `none`. `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:996-1124` (ImplicitTransTheta, incl. kernels
`m1_impl_tlim2/3`) ~129; `:1173,1694` ~30; `:165-181,467-472,568-574` ~25;
`rad_m1_implicit.hpp:80-90` 11 — removing it shrinks `M1_NHALO_T` from 14 back to 13, i.e. every
transverse halo message by one slot.

**H. `implicit_closure_relax` / `implicit_closure_relax_thin`. ~25 lines.** FAILED; the default
(1.0 / false) is a no-op. `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:149-154`, `:2507-2508` + the relaxation arithmetic in
the closure update, `:565-567`.

**I. `dbg_*` / `opac_freeze` ablations — `opac_freeze`, `dbg_gas_force`, `dbg_gas_heat`,
`dbg_gas_force_trans`, `dbg_trans_memory`. ~50 lines.** Diagnostic-only, no gate, no production
input; `dbg_trans_memory` is a known failed experiment. `rad_m1.cpp:292-302,326-335` 22;
`rad_m1_coupling.cpp` (4 sites), `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:1181` (wmem) + 2 dbg_gas_* sites,
`rad_m1_opacity.cpp` (2 opac_freeze sites) ~25.

**J. `source_form = ovc`. ~30 lines.** Self-declared "FAILING CONTROL of T4/T4b, not a physical
option". `rad_m1.cpp:257-275` 19; the `source_ovc` branch in `rad_m1_coupling.cpp` ~10.

**Removable total A-J: ~1200-1300 lines of 7776 (15-17 %)**, plus the always-allocated `ifw` device
array and one transverse-halo slot.

Listed for completeness, **NOT** proposed for removal:
- **`dbg_tensor` (frozen | tilt | tau)** — `rad_m1.cpp:300-326` + `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:2510-2511`,
  `:2736-2924`, ~200 lines. Reachable only through a switch no production sets, but it is VET
  scaffolding whose `runs_3i_tensor` verdict is a live open finding (HANDOVER-2026-09-22b).
- **`implicit_partition = gather`** — `ImplicitPartitionInit` `rad_m1_implicit.cpp:634-750` (~117),
  `ImplicitGatherSolve` `:1407-1558` (~152), plus part_* halo pieces at `:762+`, `:2559-2562` and
  the part_* members in `rad_m1.hpp:291-293` — ~300 lines. No production input sets it, but gate
  scripts do (`tests_m1/runs_3b3/run_gates_3b3.py`, `runs_3h_halo/h1.sh`), and it lifts the hard
  "one MeshBlock per x1 column" restriction.

### Box / wedge convection pgen (`src/pgen/box_convection.cpp`, 4582 lines)

Note first: **there is no MLT closure in this file** — `mlt` appears 5 times, in prose comments only;
the MLT-closure variants live in `src/pgen/red_giant.cpp`. There are no `dbg_*` names, and only two
`*_legacy` reads (`rt_cut_bc_legacy` 2231, `rt_layer_legacy` 2234, one line each, both pass-through
to `two_stream_rt.hpp`).

**A. The linear f-mode campaign: seed + projection + work integrals. ~340 lines.**
Reachable only through `seed_fmode_amp` / `fmode_hist` / `work_hist`, which no production or gate
sets, and whose campaign was explicitly retracted ("THE SEEDED LINEAR RADIATING BOX DOES NOT GROW").
`625-639` (fm_* decls + doc) 15; `1186-1396` (the whole work-integral machinery: `kNWk`,
`BoxConvWorkSnap/Close/Probe/ProbeRT`) 211; `1446-1470` (setup + fatal + probe enrolment) 25;
`1997-2000` (the `<rad_m1>` refusal) 4; `2994-3019` (seed setup, cs_top probe, startup print) 26;
`3053-3056` (the seed term in the IC kernel) 4; `4067,4073-4086` (history slot count + 11 labels)
15; `4098-4137` (fmAc/fmAs projection reduction in BoxConvHistory) 40; `4141-4145` 5.
Freeing history slots 5-15 is also what the `<rad_m1>` path is currently blocked on.

**B. The RT operator-split variants. ~385 lines firm (+73 conditional).**
Every one is a refuted / rejected / WIP way of moving the same radiation source out of the RK stage;
no production sets any of them.
`2328-2349` + `2382-2402` (rt_strang / rt_once_per_cycle / rt_col3_once setup, mutual-exclusion
fatals, `user_split_func` enrolment) 43; `2350-2381` (rt_imex setup + guards) 32; `3790-3911`
(`BoxConvRTImEx`, the ImEx implicit-stage operator) 122; `~618-622` + `4580` (`rtimex_src_` decl +
teardown) 6; `3696-3739` (`BoxConvRTSplit`) 44; `2441-2473` (rt_pair_sym setup + guards) 33;
`3583-3600` (the `rt_pair_sym == 1` ADI(bdt/2)/column/ADI(bdt/2) branches in BoxConvSrcs) 20;
`2474-2507` (rt_before_flux setup + guards) 34; `3740-3789` (`BoxConvRTBeforeFlux`) 50;
`2508-2529` (rt_split_transverse, inert unless one of the above is on) 22.
CONDITIONAL — do not cut until `rt_col3_sub` is judged: `2403-2440` (38) + the `nsub` loop at
`3571-3610` (35).

**C. Diagnostic-only / instrumentation paths. ~83 lines in this file** (the bodies are larger, in
the shared headers).
`2225` + `2697-2699` (rt_src_dump read + skip_sweep refusal) 4 — body in `two_stream_rt.hpp` ~L436
and the apply kernel; `2255-2261` (rt_impl_ablate / rt_impl_fixit + the "NOT a correct solve"
warning) 7 — bodies in `two_stream_column_implicit.hpp` / `two_stream_column_partition.hpp`;
`2530-2550` (rt_kappa_frozen setup + guard + print) 21 — body `two_stream_rt.hpp:1532` + the global
horizontal-mean reduction kernel; `2554` (rt_dump_file) 1 — **`rt_dump_m/j/k` at 2555-2557 must
STAY**, they also address the `rt_apply_debug` column the m3 gate reads; `2728-2771` (rt_force_center
+ rt_src_theta setup, range checks, skip_sweep-override warning, summary print) 44 — bodies in
`two_stream_rt.hpp` (`rt_fbsave_ptr`) and `RTCol3ThetaDe` in `two_stream_column_implicit.hpp`;
`2236-2238` (rt_outer_iter / rt_outer_verbose) 3 — multi-pass body in `two_stream_rt.hpp`
(`rt_deacc_ptr`, `rt_oconv_ptr`); keep `rt_outer_verbose` (tests_m3 reads its dump), drop
`rt_outer_iter`; `2211,2216,2217` (rt_top_re, rt_semi_lin, rt_explicit) 3 — all three are red_giant
back-compat; box_convection could assign the fixed value.

**D. Small variant branches nobody selects. ~40 lines.**
`3402-3433` (`vdb_mean_`, the plane-mean reduction of the bottom sponge behind
`vdamp_bot_mean_only`) ~32 — only ever recorded as false; `418,1876,2880,4238` (`inflow_ghost_`: a
one-line selector in BoxConvBC plus its read/print) 4 — handover says it "stays false".

**Total plausibly removable: ~810-880 lines of 4582**, of which ~340 is the f-mode/work-hist
campaign and ~385 the refuted operator splits.

Caveat carried from this slice: most `rt_*` / `rt_impl_*` names are `inline` globals **shared with
`src/pgen/red_giant.cpp`** and, for several, with `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp`. ABANDON here means
"box_convection should stop reading it"; deleting the global needs the red_giant verdict too. That
applies to rt_semi_lin, rt_explicit, rt_top_re, rt_outer_iter, rt_src_theta, rt_force_center,
rt_impl_ablate/fixit/cvfreeze, rt_col3_split_deep/_w, rt_dump_file.

### Global red giant / He4 presupernova pgen (`src/pgen/red_giant.cpp`, 5197 lines)

**NOTE: there is no FOFC switch in this file.** `fofc` appears only in the input file name
`inputs/hydro/red_giant_fofc.athinput` and in run-directory names (RG_fofc_long2/long3); FOFC itself
is a `<mesh>`/`<hydro>` option belonging to another slice.

1. **`runaway_scan` (+ `runaway_rmin` / `runaway_ratio` / `runaway_ratio_state`) — largest saving.**
   `red_giant.cpp:1582-1585` (4 reads) + 5 `runaway_scan::Scan()` call sites at `:1420,3333,3413,
   4361,4591` + the `RGNanScan` wrappers. Drags in the whole of `src/utils/runaway_scan.hpp`
   (~300 lines, header-only, not in `src/CMakeLists.txt`). Nothing in any `.athinput` or `.sh` sets
   it; its question is closed. `NOTE-2026-09-11-red-giant-switches` classes it "debugging".
   **~10 lines in red_giant.cpp + ~300 lines of runaway_scan.hpp = ~310.**

2. **`problem/e_ledger` — the per-operator energy ledger.** Graded KEEP in the table only because
   tests_r12 arms 12-16 set it, but the code is large and the question is answered.
   `:4609-4634` (el_[3], el_e0_, el_t0_, `RgTotE` + its parallel_reduce), `:4698-4730` (el_tsum_,
   `RgShellAcc`), `:4764-4790` (`RedGiantRTSplitLedger` + the per-shell text dump), plus `:4687-4690`
   inside `RedGiantGravityLedger` and `:4829`. **`RedGiantGravityLedger` itself (`:4686-4692`) must
   STAY** — cs_max dispatches through it. **~90 lines.**

3. **`problem/rt_no_heat`** — diagnostic "adiabatic star with the force law intact".
   `:1430` (read) + `:4732-4762` (the `rt_nh_save_` DvceArray5D allocation, the save and restore
   kernels, the `RedGiantRTSplit` dispatch wrapper). Removing it collapses `RedGiantRTSplit` into a
   direct call. No input or script ever sets true. **~33 lines** plus a 5-D scratch array.

4. **The per-column MLT closure (`mlt_mean = false`) and its tapers `mlt_rmax`, `mlt_tau_lo/hi`.**
   `:3442-3464` (the whole `if (!mltmean)` `par_for("rg_mlt_tau")` optical-depth march and the
   `taumlt` array), `:3427` (rmax_mlt), `:3430` (lgtlo/lgthi); the per-column branches in the
   deposit kernel at `:3863` (mlt_rmax cut) and `:3898-3905` (the wtau taper); decls `:594,604-605`;
   reads `:1516-1518`. Every production runs `mlt_mean = true`; the per-column form has two measured
   fatal defects (R1b_mlt / R1c_mlt) recorded in its own declaration comment, and
   `inputs/hydro/he4_presn_sp.athinput:355` states outright that "mlt_tau_lo/hi … are UNUSED under
   mlt_mean". **~45-55 lines** plus the `taumlt` allocation.

5. **`problem/wb_grav_source = "plain"`** — explicitly "Not a production option" in its own comment
   (`:190-197`). `:1563-1577` (string parse + fatal), `:197` (decl), `:3357` (wbplain capture) and
   the two negated conditions at `:3378,3381`. No hits in any `.athinput`/`.sh`/`.txt` under
   `tests_*/` or `inputs/`. **~22 lines**, and it simplifies the WB gravity kernel's branching.

6. **`problem/mlt_flux_fix` (+ `_cap`, `_rmax`) — REFUTED experiment.**
   `:1531,1534-1535` (reads), `:636-643` + `:713` (decls), `:3612-3613` (kernel captures
   ffix/ffcap/ffrmax) and the signed-residual branch at ~`:3752-3760`. `tests_r11/RESULTS-0919.md`:
   "REFUTED: 'thermal imbalance is the driver, fix the flux': ffx3 expands the same …, L_out/L drops
   to 0.96-0.98". **~28 lines.**

7. **`problem/open_budget`** — the three-pass open-BC energy ledger, superseded by `face_budget`.
   `:2688` (read), `:330-334` (decl comment), `:442-444` (open_dE_ent_/prs_/den_ + their `_l_`
   shadows and open_t_last_), `:4223-4248` (accumulate + report). Secondary saving: with it gone,
   `dEent`/`dEprs` (`:4086,4138,4193-4195`) have no consumer and can leave the open-BC
   parallel_reduce — but **`dEden` MUST stay**, `open_conserve` uses it at `:4200`.
   `inputs/hydro/red_giant_fofc.athinput:228` sets `open_budget = 0` and `:229` `face_budget = 200`.
   **~30 lines directly, ~45 with the reduction slots.**

8. **`problem/opac_compare`** — the one-shot CK-derived Rosseland table dump.
   `:2167-2204`: saves the conduction object's table views, flips `rad_kappa_rho`, calls
   `ck::ck_build_rosseland_table(pc)`, mirrors and writes the file, restores everything. Blank in all
   four repo inputs, no gate, no recorded result. **`correlated_k::ck_build_rosseland_table` itself
   must STAY** — `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:1669` calls it. **~38 lines.**

**Total realistically removable from `src/pgen/red_giant.cpp`: ~290-320 lines of 5197**, plus
deleting `src/utils/runaway_scan.hpp` (~300 lines) outright.

### Well-balanced / cubed-sphere / polar family

1. **`wb_option` variants nothing selects.** Every production uses `polytropic`; the only CI gate
   (`tst/test_suite/hydro/test_hydro_fofc_wb_cpu.py`) uses `isentropic`. Unreachable in practice:
   `isodensity`, `isothermal`, `isentropic_dt`, `adaptive`, `adaptive_fast`.
   `src/utils/wb_background.hpp:155-186` (switch arms + adaptive entropy comparison, ~30 lines),
   `:287-311` (wb_opt==4, ~25), `:352-357` (wb_opt==0, ~6), `:358-365` (wb_opt==1, ~8);
   parse arms `src/hydro/hydro.cpp:220-243` and `src/mhd/mhd.cpp:245-268` (~20).
   **~60-85 lines + ~20.** Caveat: `wb_opt == 1` is also the `default:` return of `WBOptionNumber`,
   so its walk branch cannot go without re-choosing the fallback.

2. **`wellbalance_static` / `wellbalance_static_reconst`** — the older static-background scheme.
   No production sets either; only the hydro CI gate and 4 pgens
   (`deep_hot_jupiter.cpp`, `convection.cpp`, `solid_body_rot.cpp`, `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp`) do.
   `src/hydro/hydro_wellbalance.cpp` (397 lines), `src/mhd/mhd_wellbalance.cpp` (265 lines — the MHD
   half has NO gate and NO production at all, so it is truly unreachable). Allocation blocks
   `hydro.cpp:260-278`, `mhd.cpp:285-303`; task hooks `hydro_tasks.cpp:328,459,478`,
   `mhd_tasks.cpp:202,243,265`; perturbation paths `hydro_fluxes.cpp:123`, `hydro_fofc.cpp:221`,
   `mhd_fluxes.cpp:119`, `mhd_fofc.cpp:360`.
   **~300 lines (MHD half only, safe) / ~700 if the hydro half goes too** (the latter breaks the CI
   gate and 4 pgens).

3. **`wb_x3` (hydro + mhd)** — set by no production, no gate.
   `src/hydro/hydro_fluxes.cpp:708-744`, `src/mhd/mhd_fluxes.cpp:724-772` (the
   `WbLocalPiecewiseLinearX3` call sites) + the X3 overloads in `hydro.hpp`/`mhd.hpp`.
   **~150-200 lines.** It is the symmetric sibling of the CI-gated `wb_x2`, so removal buys little.

4. **`cs_diag_no_coordsrc` / `cs_diag_no_divf` / `cs_diag_no_magsrc`** — RHS-split ablation
   diagnostics from the closed cs-MHD-instability investigation; `cs_diag_no_magsrc` is recorded as
   NOT a usable probe (the O(1) inconsistency sends KE ballistic).
   `mhd.cpp:342-345`, `mhd_tasks.cpp:337`, `mhd_update.cpp:45`,
   `coordinates.cpp:43,1363,1490,1986`, `coordinates.hpp:188-189,198`.
   **~20 lines**, but they sit in three hot kernels' capture lists.

5. **`cs_corner_poison`** — debug 1e30 poison of the cube-vertex corner ghosts; the experiment was
   run and answered. `mesh/mesh.cpp:131-133`, `mesh.hpp:305`, `bvals_cc.cpp:954,1006`. **~10 lines.**

6. **`cs_seam_diag`** — measurement instrument for a hypothesis that was RETRACTED.
   Whole file `src/mhd/mhd_seam_diag.cpp` (**207 lines**) + `mhd.cpp:404-413`,
   `mhd_tasks.cpp:754-755`, `mhd.hpp:224`. **~225 lines.**

7. **`cs_seam_econsist`** — measured marginally WORSE (seam L1(v) 1.6405e-3 -> 1.6418e-3) and fatals
   on more than one rank, so no production could ever use it. Whole file
   `src/mhd/mhd_seam_econsist.cpp` (**162 lines**) + `mhd.cpp:414-445` (~32),
   `mhd_tasks.cpp:130,684-698,779,843` (~40, incl. two task functions), `mhd.hpp:230`.
   **~235 lines.**

8. **`cs_lowbeta_diag`** — measurement only; set by nothing, named in no note or gate.
   `mhd.cpp:375-401` (~27), `mhd_fluxes.cpp:100-107,956,976-1020` (`ReportLowBetaDiag`, ~60), the
   `diag/ibin0/nbin/lbd` parameters and atomic counters in
   `rsolvers/cs_lowbeta_fallback.hpp:79,110-114`, `mhd.hpp:235-242`. **~110 lines**, and it is the
   only reason the low-beta rsolver carries four extra kernel arguments.

9. **`use_polar_average_b`** — measured to do NOTHING for the sp polar field blow-up (four of its
   "identical" gates were false positives against a binary that had no such path).
   `mesh.cpp:231`, `mesh.hpp:332`, `bfield_bcs.cpp:216-219` + `PolarAzimuthalAverageBxBy`
   `bfield_bcs.cpp:310-400`, declaration `bvals.hpp:243`. **~100 lines.**

10. **`use_polar_average_eresist` — NOT dead.** Default ON and load-bearing (without it polar-row
    div B grows to ~1e-3 b0/L). Only the `false` branch is untaken; nothing to remove.

11. **`polar_hlle_rows` variants** — default 6 (x2+x3) is the adopted fix; the x1 bit (mask 1/7) is
    the sp MHD energy excess itself; 0 is the pre-fix behaviour. Only the legacy `"true"`/`"false"`
    spellings at `mhd.cpp:368-369` are strictly dead (**~2 lines**). The bitmask is a legitimate
    control — keep.

12. **`polar_x3_shift` variants** — default `rotate` is the fix; `off`, `shift`, `scalar` are the
    bisect-era alternatives nothing sets. `mesh.cpp:212-221` (~6) plus the `px3_ == 2 / == 3`
    branches in `hydro_fluxes.cpp:~783-820` and `mhd_fluxes.cpp:~828-870`. **~80-120 lines**
    (estimate — the two branch bodies were not read in full).

13. **`sp_cart_polar_momentum` / `cs_cart_momentum`** — both recorded as measured negatives
    ("stays as a measured negative (keep off)"; "does NOTHING on this evolved test, one-cycle force
    0.14 -> 0.39, worse"). `coordinates.cpp:1368,1494,2220-2234,2303-2316` + the Cartesian-triad
    helpers around `coordinates.cpp:1820-1840,2100-2340`. Entangled with `sp_cart_all_momentum`
    (a measured win, never adopted), so **~60 lines** if that one is kept, several hundred if the
    whole Cartesian-momentum family goes.

**Family subtotal: ~1100-1300 lines safely removable**, dominated by items 6, 7, 8, 2 (MHD half)
and 9.


# Method, and what could not be determined

## Method
- Parameter set: every `pin->GetOrAdd*` / `Get*` / `DoesParameterExist` read in
  `src/utils/two_stream_rt.hpp`, `two_stream_column_{ck,implicit,partition}.hpp`,
  `two_stream_warm_rst.hpp`, `correlated_k.hpp`, `atm_column.hpp`, `wb_background.hpp`,
  `src/diffusion/conduction{,_transverse}.cpp`, `src/rad_m1/*.cpp`,
  `src/pgen/{deep_hot_jupiter_rt,box_convection,red_giant}.cpp`, plus the WB / cubed-sphere / polar
  reads in `src/hydro/hydro.cpp`, `src/mhd/mhd.cpp`, `src/coordinates/coordinates.cpp`,
  `src/mesh/mesh.cpp`. 529 read sites, **432 distinct parameters**. Note that the RT headers
  themselves contain **no** `pin->` reads: they declare `inline` globals which the three pgens set.
- `introduced` column: `git log -S'"<name>"' --format='%h %ad %s' -- src | tail -1`, i.e. the oldest
  commit that changed a line containing the quoted key. It is the introducing commit for a key that
  was never renamed; a renamed key shows the rename, not the original idea.
- Restart-file compatibility flags are not counted as switches and were not collected.

## Known gaps
- **`rot_potential`** appears once, via `deep_hot_jupiter_rt.cpp:769`. If `red_giant.cpp` or
  `box_convection.cpp` also honour a rotational potential they do it under a different key.
- Line counts throughout the DEAD CODE section are estimates. The two least precise are
  `rad_blend_use_2s` and the `rad_x1_verbose` audit (both quoted 150-180), and the split of the
  interleaved `(b2)` block in `rad_m1_implicit.cpp` between `implicit_flux` and `implicit_recon`.
- The mode-1/2 footprint of `rt_implicit_column` is interleaved with the live mode-3 path inside
  `rt_apply` and the two chain kernels; the quoted ranges are where the code lives, not a clean
  excision.
- `rad_m1/implicit_eos_cache` (+ `_nt`, `_check`) and `rad_m1/implicit_opac_update` are left UNSURE:
  the first has a PASS verdict in `tests_m1/runs_3g_newton_T` and is the main measured speed-up, but
  it is an approximation (frozen rho, T-interpolated EOS) that no production has used; the second is
  a physics call about strongly T-dependent table opacity that the docs do not settle.
- `rad_m1/implicit_bc_x1min`, `implicit_flux_x1max`, `implicit_ebath_x1max` and `kappa_unit` have no
  status evidence anywhere; they are KEEP only because their partners are set by the M1 slab input.
- `problem/rt_budget_verbose` is diagnostic-only and no input sets it, but `bench/m1_stage2/PLAN.md`
  reserves its slots 10-13 for the M1 energy budget. KEEP, but it is a user call.
- `problem/stellar_tide` is a rule collision: validated physics (c4aa730f, 0.5 % at the limb) set by
  no production and no gate. UNSURE by design — a physics decision, not a code-health one.
- `hydro`-vs-`mhd` mirrors (`wb_x2`, `wb_rho`, `wb_rmax`, `wb_rmin`) are KEEP purely as mirrors of
  CI-gated / production-set hydro switches; if the two modules may diverge, all four are ABANDON.
- `kappa_iso`, `kappa_iso_limit` and `isotropic_conduction` are upstream AthenaK surface, not local
  experiments; they were not diffed against upstream `main`.
- Only one `bench/` tree exists, at `/viper/u2/jinma/ATHENAK/bench`, a sibling of the checkout.
